+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+

+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {

@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();

+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};

+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};

+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,

+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);

+        }

+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();

+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {

@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));

+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;

+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;

+class CudaPricer {

+    CudaBatch batches[2];

+    unsigned maxBlocks;

+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,149 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 256;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount) * 32;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

     constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();

 // Standard test cases for validation
 inline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {
     return {{
@@ -92,6 +56,154 @@
     }};
 }
 
+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaBatch {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    double* device = nullptr;
+    double* host = nullptr;
+    size_t offset = 0;
+    size_t count = 0;
+
+    CudaBatch() = default;
+    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaBatch() {
+        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
+        if (device) cudaFree(device);
+        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
+        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
+    }
+};
+
+class CudaPricer {
+    // Bounded, double-buffered storage also supports inputs larger than GPU RAM.
+    static constexpr size_t batchCapacity = 1 << 20;
+    static constexpr unsigned threadsPerBlock = 512;
+    CudaBatch batches[2];
+    size_t capacity;
+    unsigned maxBlocks;
+    unsigned numBatches;
+
+public:
+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        int activeBlocks;
+        // Load the kernel during setup and keep several waves of resident blocks.
+        checkCuda(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
+                      &activeBlocks, priceOptions, threadsPerBlock, 0),
+                  "cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount * activeBlocks) * 8;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
+

+    explicit CudaPricer(size_t count) : capacity(std::min(count, batchCapacity)) {
+        int device;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDevice(&device), "cudaGetDevice");
+        cudaDeviceProp properties;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceProperties(&properties, device), "cudaGetDeviceProperties");
+        int activeBlocks;
+        // Load the kernel during setup and keep several waves of resident blocks.
+        checkCuda(cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor(
+                      &activeBlocks, priceOptions, threadsPerBlock, 0),
+                  "cudaOccupancyMaxActiveBlocksPerMultiprocessor");
+        maxBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(properties.multiProcessorCount * activeBlocks) * 8;
+
+        constexpr auto options = getTestOptions();
+        std::array<PricingTerms, numBaseOptions> terms{};
+        for (size_t i = 0; i < options.size(); ++i) {
+            const auto& option = options[i];
+            terms[i] = {option.spot, option.strike,
+                        (option.r - option.q + 0.5 * option.vol * option.vol) * option.t,
+                        option.t <= 0.0 || option.vol <= 0.0 ? 0.0 : option.vol * sqrt(option.t),
+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};

    59	// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
    60	struct PricingTerms {
    61	    double spot;
    62	    double strike;
    63	    double drift;
    64	    double stddev;
    65	    double spotDiscount;
    66	    double strikeDiscount;
    67	    int type;
    68	};
    69	
    70	constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
    71	__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
    72	
    73	__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
    74	    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
    75	}
    76	
    77	__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
    78	                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
    79	    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
    80	        return 0.0;
    81	    }
    82	    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
    83	    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
    84	    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
    85	    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
    86	    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
    87	    if (option.type == CALL) {
    88	        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
    89	    }
    90	    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
    91	}
    92	
    93	// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
    94	__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
    95	    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    96	    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    97	         j < count; j += stride) {
    98	        const size_t i = offset + j;
    99	        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
   100	        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
   101	        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
   102	        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
   103	    }
   104	}

   106	void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
   107	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
   108	        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
   109	    }
   110	}

   112	struct CudaBatch {
   113	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   114	    double* device = nullptr;
   115	    double* host = nullptr;
   116	    size_t offset = 0;
   117	    size_t count = 0;

   119	    CudaBatch() = default;
   120	    CudaBatch(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
   121	    CudaBatch& operator=(const CudaBatch&) = delete;
   122	
   123	    ~CudaBatch() {
   124	        if (stream) cudaStreamSynchronize(stream);
   125	        if (device) cudaFree(device);
   126	        if (host) cudaFreeHost(host);
   127	        if (stream) cudaStreamDestroy(stream);

   207	// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set
   208	void generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {
   209	    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();
   210	    options.resize(numOptions);
   211	    
   212	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {
   213	        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly
   214	        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];
   215	        options[i] = base;
   216	        
   217	        // Add some variation for larger datasets
   218	        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));
   219	        options[i].spot *= factor;
   220	        options[i].strike *= factor;
   221	    }
   222	}

+// These terms do not change when the generator scales spot and strike.
+struct PricingTerms {
+    double spot;
+    double strike;
+    double drift;
+    double stddev;
+    double spotDiscount;
+    double strikeDiscount;
+    int type;
+};
+
+constexpr size_t numBaseOptions = getTestOptions().size();
+__constant__ PricingTerms deviceOptions[numBaseOptions];
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double cumulativeNormal(double x) noexcept {
+    return 0.5 * (1.0 + erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ double blackScholes(const PricingTerms& option,
+                                              double S, double K) noexcept {
+    if (option.stddev <= 0.0) {
+        return 0.0;
+    }
+    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + option.drift) / option.stddev;
+    const double d2 = d1 - option.stddev;
+    const double sign = option.type == CALL ? 1.0 : -1.0;
+    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d1);
+    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(sign * d2);
+    if (option.type == CALL) {
+        return S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1 - K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2;
+    }
+    return K * option.strikeDiscount * Nd2 - S * option.spotDiscount * Nd1;
+}
+
+// Fuse the original input generation and pricing, with coalesced result stores.
+__global__ void priceOptions(double* __restrict__ results, size_t count, size_t offset) {
+    const size_t stride = static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
+    for (size_t j = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+         j < count; j += stride) {
+        const size_t i = offset + j;
+        const PricingTerms& option = deviceOptions[i % numBaseOptions];
+        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(numBaseOptions));
+        // Scale before dividing: rounded S/K must match the original generator.
+        results[j] = blackScholes(option, option.spot * factor, option.strike * factor);
+    }

+                        exp(-option.q * option.t), exp(-option.r * option.t), option.type};
+        }
+        // This also initializes CUDA for an empty workload; there is no CPU fallback.
+        checkCuda(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(deviceOptions, terms.data(), sizeof(terms)),
+                  "cudaMemcpyToSymbol");
+
+        numBatches = count > capacity ? 2 : (count != 0 ? 1 : 0);
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) {
+            auto& batch = batches[i];
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&batch.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                      "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+            checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.device), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMalloc");
+            checkCuda(cudaMallocHost(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&batch.host), capacity * sizeof(double)),
+                      "cudaMallocHost");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void price(std::vector<double>& results) {
+        auto collect = [&](CudaBatch& batch) {
+            if (batch.count != 0) {
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(batch.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+                std::memcpy(results.data() + batch.offset, batch.host, batch.count * sizeof(double));
+            }
+        };
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        unsigned nextBatch = 0;
+        while (offset < results.size()) {
+            auto& batch = batches[nextBatch];
+            collect(batch);
+            batch.offset = offset;
+            batch.count = std::min(capacity, results.size() - offset);
+            const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min(
+                (batch.count + threadsPerBlock - 1) / threadsPerBlock,
+                static_cast<size_t>(maxBlocks)));
+            priceOptions<<<blocks, threadsPerBlock, 0, batch.stream>>>(
+                batch.device, batch.count, offset);
+            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError(), "priceOptions launch");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(batch.host, batch.device, batch.count * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, batch.stream), "cudaMemcpyAsync");
+            offset += batch.count;
+            nextBatch = (nextBatch + 1) % numBatches;
+        }
+        for (unsigned i = 0; i < numBatches; ++i) collect(batches[i]);
+    }
+};
